7～9 月期実質GDP は年率＋1.0％と 
プラス成長を予想（1 次QE）

○ 2026年7～9月期の実質GDP（1次速報値）は前期比＋0.2％（年率＋1.0％）と、4～6月期（前期比＋
0.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半期連続でのプラス成長を予想 
○ 個人消費が伸び悩む一方、設備投資や民間在庫が押し上げる見込み。なお、公的在庫と輸入では
中東情勢を主因とする一時的な変動が生じ、成長率にプラス・マイナス両面で影響 
○ 年度後半の日本経済は、小幅なプラス成長を予想。インフレ再加速を受け消費抑制が続く一方、設
備投資が堅調に拡大。日本銀行は12月会合で政策金利を1.50％に引き上げる見込み

7～9月期の実質GDP（1次
速報値）はプラス成長 を
予想 
11月16日 （月） に、 内閣府から2026年7～9月期の 「四半期別GDP速 報（QE）」
（1次速報値）が公表される。現時点で入手可能な基礎統計の結果を踏まえ
ると、7～9月期の実質GDPは前期比＋0.2％（ 年 率＋1.0％） となる見込みだ。
4～6月期の前期比＋0.4％（年率＋1.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半
期連続でプラス成長になると予想する（図表）。 
7～9月期は、内需の柱である個人消費が伸び悩む一方、 設備投資の拡大や、
製造業を中心とする在庫積み増しが成長率を押し上げるとみられる。その他
では、前期の備蓄原油放出の反動が公的在庫投資のプラス要因として現れる
が、原油の代替調達進展を反映した輸入の増加がマイナス要因になるなど、

前期比に対する寄与度（％Pt）

### Page 2

中東情勢による一時的な変動も引き続き成長率に影響する見込みだ。以下、
需要項目別に7～9月期の経済動向を詳しく確認しよう。 
個人消費は伸び悩み 。耐
久財の特需剥落や、 根強
い節約志向が影響 
個人消費は前期比▲0.1％（4～6月期：同＋0.0％） と、2四半期連続で伸び
悩む結果になろう。消費の弱さの背景には、 複合的な要因があると考えられ
る。 
耐久財では、自動車とエアコンの「特需」剥落が押し下げ要因になろう。
自動車は、購入時にかかる自動車税の環境性能割が3月末に廃止されたこと
を受け、4月から自動車販売が過去平均に比べ上振れていたが、8月以降は再
び過去平均並みの水準に戻っている。エアコンは、2027年4月の省エネ基準
引き上げで販売価格が上昇するとの見方から駆け込み需要が生じていたが、
景気ウォッチャー調査のコメントなどをみると、8月に入った後は一服した
模様だ。9月は気温が低かったほか、長雨による客足の減少もエアコンなど
季節家電の売り上げに悪影響を及ぼしたとみられる。 
食品やエネルギー、 各種消耗品などの非耐久財では、物価高を背景とする
根強い節約志向により、 弱含みが続いているようだ。今回のインフレ局面が
本格化する前の2020年と比較すると、非耐久消費財の物価は足元までの約6
年半でおよそ1.3倍の水準に上昇した計算になる。こうした累積的な価格上
昇が、 消費者の体感物価の高まりを通じて節約志向を強め、 購入数量の抑制
につながっている可能性がある。 
サービス消費は、 イベント関連支出などで底堅さがみられる一方、日常的
な支出には物価高による節約志向が波及し、 サービスや価格帯、 顧客層によ
る二極化が進みつつあるとみられる。 また、8～9月には台風・長雨による旅
行・ 宿泊のキャンセルや外出控えの影響から、 レジャー関連を中心に消費が
伸び悩んだようだ。 
設備投 資は3四半期ぶり
に前期比プラス 
設備投資は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.9％） と、3四半期ぶりに前期
比プラスへ転じると予想する。 
直近の設備投資関連指標をみると、資本財の国内向け供給量を表す資本財
総供給指数（除く輸送機械）が7～8月に大きく上昇した。また第3次産業活
動指数では、ソフトウェア業の実質売上高が同じく7～8月に増加したことが
確認でき、どちらも7～9月期の設備投資が拡大したことを示唆している。 
加えて、前期の4～6月期に発生した一時的な押し下げ圧力（特許権の海外
譲渡による国内研究開発投資の減少） がなくなったこともプラス要因だ。た
だし、 これは裏を返せば、7～9月期のQEにおける設備投資の伸びが実態以上
に強い数字になるということでもあり、評価する際には留意が必要である。 
住宅投資は 前期比マイナ
スが継続 
住宅投資は前期比▲0.8％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.6％ ）と、2四半期連続で前期
比マイナスとなろう。中東情勢を背景とする資材不足は徐々に緩和している
とみられるが、7～8月の住宅着工件数が伸び悩んでおり、住宅価格や金利上

### Page 3

3

昇による需要低迷に加えて、 足元では地震や台風などによる着工 ・ 進捗の遅
れが発生している可能性がある。 
民間 在庫は 2四半期連続
で成長率にプラス寄与 
民間在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は、＋0.2％Pt（4～6月期：
＋0.3％Pt）とプラスが続く見込みだ。AI関連需要の強さなどを背景に、製
造業で在庫積み増しの動きが進んでいることが影響すると考えられる。な
お、4～6月期は石油化学製品などの供給不足を懸念した前倒し発注が在庫を
押し上げたとみられるが、7～9月期は原油の代替調達進展に伴って供給不足
が緩和したことで、そうした要因の影響は一巡した模様だ。 
公的需要 は備蓄原油放出
の反動で前期比プラスに 
公的需要は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.8％）と、2四半期ぶりの前期
比プラスを予想する。 
最大の要因は、公的在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度がプラスに
転じるとみられることだ。4～6月期は国家備蓄原油の放出により公的在庫変
動の寄与度が▲0.5％Ptと大幅マイナスになったが、7～9月期は国家原油備
蓄の減少が小幅なものにとどまり（6月末：105日分→9月末：102日分）、公
的在庫の減少ペースが緩和することで、成長率に対し＋0.3％Pt程度のプラ
ス寄与になると見込まれる。 
その他の公的需要項目では、政府消費を前期比＋0.2％（4～6月期：同＋
1.7％）と予想する。4～6月期は高校授業料・小学校給食費の無償化開始に
より高い伸びになったが、7～9月期はそうした特殊要因が剥落し、緩やかな
増加ペースに戻ると考えられる。公共投資は前期比▲0.5％（4～6月期：同
▲0.5％）と、土木関連工事の減少を受けて2四半期連続のマイナスを見込む。 
輸出が小幅に減少する 一
方、 輸入が増加し、外需は
4四半期ぶりのマイナス
寄与 
外需では、輸出が前期比▲0.3％（4～6月期：同＋0.4％）と小幅に減少す
る一方、輸 入が同＋1.3％（4～6月 期：同▲1.7％）と増加に転じる見込みだ。
実質GDP成長率に対する外需の寄与度は▲0.3％Ptと、4四半期ぶりのマイナ
スが予想される。 
輸入の増加は、原油の代替調達進展が主因だ。中東情勢の緊迫化により4
～6月期は一時的に原油輸入が急減したが、7～9月期は米国産原油やホルム
ズ海峡を通過しない中東産原油の調達が増え、9月を除いて前年並みの輸入
量が確保されている。 
輸入はGDPの控除項目であることから、7～9月期は輸入増加が成長率の下
押し要因になる。 ただし、代替調達の進展に伴って国家備蓄原油の放出が抑
制され、 先述したように7～9月期は公的在庫変動が成長率にプラス寄与する
とみられることから、輸入増による成長率押し下げ効果の一部は公的在庫変
動により相殺されるだろう。 
輸出では、前期の4～6月期に特許権の海外譲渡がサービス輸出を押し上げ
た （これは4～6月期の設備投資の押し下げ要因でもある） 反動が現れ、 一時
的に下振れするだろう。一方、旺盛なAI関連需要が支えになり、半導体・電

### Page 4

4

子部品や半導体製造装置などの財輸出は堅調に推移する見込みだ。インバウ
ンド需要 （非居住者家計の国内での直接購入） は、 日中対立の継続による中
国人訪日客の少なさに加え、 航空運賃上昇を受けた欧米訪日客の伸び悩みに
より、低調な結果になると見込まれる。 
2026 年度後半 の実質 GDP
は小幅プラス と予想 。個
人消費が伸び悩む一方、
設備投資が堅調拡大 
以上のとおり、7～9月期の日本経済は前期から減速するものの、4四半期
連続でプラス成長になると予想する。GDPの約半分を占める個人消費の伸び
悩みが足を引っ張っているが、設備投資や製造業を中心とする在庫積み増し
がけん引する格好だ。 
先行きについてみると、2026年度後半（2026年10～2027年3月）の 実質GDP
は、 均してみれば小幅なプラス成長になると予想する。個人消費が振るわな
い一方で、 設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が続くだろう。2026年度全体の
実質GDP成長率は、前年度末の堅調な伸びで今年度の平均水準が押し上げら
れることもあり（いわゆるプラスのゲタ）、前年比＋1％程度とまずまずの
成長率になる計算だ。 
年度後半の個人消費では、インフレ再加速による実質賃金の伸び悩みが消
費拡大の抑制要因になるだろう。 原油 ・ ナフサ価格高騰の影響が徐々に波及
し、 コア消費者物価指数 （生鮮食品を除く総合） は年度末にかけて前年比＋
3％程度まで上昇すると予想される（ただし、2026年末から政府の電気・ガ
ス料金支援策が実施されれば、0.4～0.5％Pt程度の押し下げ要因になる可能
性がある）。加えて、2027年4月の消費税減税開始前には、保存食品などを
中心とした買い控えの動きが生じるとみられ、 一時的に消費が落ち込む可能
性がある。 
他方で、年度後半の設備投資は堅調に拡大するだろう。日銀短観（9月調
査） では、2026年度の設備投資計画 （ソフトウェア・研究開発を含む設備投
資額 （除く土地投資額）） が前年比＋9.3％と高い伸びを維持した （6月 調 査：
同＋8.8％）。生産・営業用設備投資判断DIが2025年度後半以降に低下し、
設備の不足感が強まっていることをあわせて考えれば、 設備投資は今後拡大
傾向で推移していく見込みだ。 
また、政府の投資支援策である「大胆な投資促進税制」 （特定生産性向上
設備等投資促進税制）が2026年7月末から開始されたことも、今後の設備投
資にとってプラス要因になろう。同税制における減税規模の政府見込みを参
考に試算すると、2026～2028年度に設備投資の前年比変化率を約0.1％Ptず
つ押し上げる効果があると考えられる。 
ただし、 日銀が来年にかけて政策金利を一段と引き上げ、 企業の新規借入
金利がさらに上昇すると予想されること、 また、 投資財の価格が上昇傾向を
続けると考えられることを踏まえると、 中小企業を中心に設備投資を控える
企業が今後増加する可能性がある点には注意が必要だ。

### Page 5

5

日銀は10月会合で 政策金
利を据え置き 。次回利上
げは12月と予想 
10月29・30日に開催された金融政策決定会合において、 日本銀行は政策金
利を現行の1.25％で据え置いた。次の利上げ（1.25％→1.50％） は、次回12
月17・18日の会合と予想する。 前回利上げを行った9月会合に続いて 「3カ月
に1回」ペースでの利上げになり、 日銀の利上げペースが6月以前の 「半年強
に1回」から加速していることを印象付けるものになろう。 
企業の価格転嫁行動は一段と積極化しており、植田総裁が9月会合後の記
者会見で 「政策の局面が変化した」と説明したように、日本銀行は「物価引
き上げ」 から 「物価上振れ抑制」 へと金融政策運営の舵を切っている。12月
の追加利上げ後も、 金利上昇の影響を確認しながら、 日銀は最終的に2027年
半ばにかけて政策金利を2.0％まで引き上げていくだろう。 
なお、経済見通しの詳細については、みずほ総合研究所調査部「2026年・
2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し ─ 転換点は近いのか、投資主導経済の行方
を探る ─」（10月2日）をご覧いただきたい。 
（https://www.mizuhobank.co.jp/corporate/mhri/research/report/pdf/outlook261002.pdf）

Document 105: 261027_ms_lotes-UG

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 19
Status: ok

### Page 1

M
Idea
3Q GM likely to decline on higher costs and slower new product adoption…
Lotes reported unaudited 3Q26 revenue of NT$9,924M (+4% q/q, +18% y/y). Higher
copper, gold and plastics costs for legacy products, coupled with slower new
product adoption, should continue to pressure GM. We forecast 3Q GM of 44.6% (-
90bps q/q), below consensus of 45.8% (+30bps q/q). However, a decline in opex to
NT$2.0B, consistent with management commentary, should support operating

profit growth of 17% q/q to NT$2,452M, albeit still 6% below consensus. We
forecast NP of NT$2,174M (+10% q/q) and EPS of NT$19.36, 4% below the Street.
…but we see 3Q as the GM trough, with recovery beginning in 4Q: Following
slower  server platform transitions, we expect adoption to accelerate from 4Q26.
Socket production for AMD's Venice CPU will ramp from 4Q26 and socket
production for Intel’s Oak Stream CPU will ramp in 1Q27. These transitions should

enable Lotes to reprice products and pass through higher input costs, supporting
margin recovery. Our industry checks also point to a rebound in ODM server board
production in 4Q, following a modest 3Q decline. We forecast 4Q revenue of ~NT
$10.5B (+5% q/q), GM of 45.9% (+130bps q/q) and EPS of NT$21.11 (+9% q/q).
2027 - margin recovery and new AI product ramps should drive earnings growth:
Rising adoption of new CPU platforms should support socket margin recovery,

alongside growing contributions from QD, SOCAMM and NPO. QD reached 2–3% of
revenue in September, with further growth expected in 4Q26 and 2027, and we
forecast SOCAMM will reach ~7% of revenue in CY27. Lotes has two NPO
opportunities, one through LINTES and another directly, with the direct program
expected to ramp in 2H27. Although initial contributions should remain modest, we
see NPO as a longer-term AI data center growth opportunity and a potential re-

rating catalyst. Overall, we see the AI revenue mix, including QD, SOCAMM and
NPO, rising from ~5% in CY25 to slightly above 20% by CY28.
Upgrade to OW and raise PT by 15% to NT$2,350, driven by our estimate increases
for CY27-28. Our new PT implies 22x 2027e P/E and 16.5x 2028e P/E, above the past
3-year average of 17.4x, which we view as reasonable amid a rising AI revenue mix.
What’s Changed
Lotes Co. Ltd. (3533.TW) From To
Rating Equal-weight Overweight

Price Target NT$2,045.00 NT$2,350.00
Lotes Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Margins to trough in 3Q;
Upgrade to OW
We look  beyond near-term cost pressures to margin recovery
from 4Q as new CPU platforms enable better cost pass-through.
AI products such as QD, SOCAMM and NPO should lift the AI
revenue mix to >20% by 2028, supporting earnings growth and
re-rating.

3Q26 Results Preview
3Q26 preview:  We forecast revenue of NT$9.92B (+4% q/q, +18% y/y), broadly in line
with the Street. Although gross margin should decline 80bps q/q to 44.6%, a 12%
sequential reduction in operating expenses should lift operating margin 270bps q/q to
24.7%. Nevertheless, this remains 130bps below consensus, implying operating profit of
NT$2.45bn, 6% below the Street. We therefore see potential for an earnings miss, as we

estimate net profit of ~NT$2,174M (+10% q/q, -6% y/y), for EPS of NT$19.36, 4% below
the Street.

AI revenue mix - a sharp step-up in 2027 should strengthen the structural growth
story: We forecast AI revenue contribution will rise from ~5% in CY25  to slightly above

20% by CY28, with the largest increase occurring in 2027. Rising adoption of QD and
SOCAMM connectors should drive the initial acceleration, while NPO sockets provide an
additional growth opportunity, with small-volume contributions expected in 2027 and
larger volumes in 2028. We believe this expanding AI exposure, alongside expected margin
recovery in CPU sockets, should strengthen Lotes’ earnings growth profile and support a
potential valuation re-rating as investors look beyond near-term cost pressures.

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
Price Target Discussion and Valuation Methodology
Our price target increases ~15%, to NT$2,350 from NT$2,045, driven mainly by our
higher earnings estimates for 2027-28.
Our new PT implies ~22x 2027e P/E, slightly above its three-year average forward P/E of
17.4x, but still below that of peer Amphenol (25-35x 2027e P/E, based on Visible Alpha
consensus). We think Lotes stock could  re-rate to a higher valuation multiple if margins
hold up well and Lotes' exposure to AI PCs and AI servers grows in the coming years.

We continue to derive our PT, which is our base case scenario value, from a residual
income model (RIM), consistent with the methodology we apply to the rest of our tech
hardware coverage universe. To calculate the intrinsic value of the stock, we use the
company's beginning equity plus the present value of all expected residual income of
earnings in excess of the cost of capital. The RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of
capital and negative when it is below the cost of capital.

Our key RIM assumptions (all unchanged) include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of ~1.0,
equity risk premium of ~8.7% and risk-free rate of ~1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of
12.5% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Our bull and bear case values rise to NT$3,400 and NT1,285, respectively, in line with
the increase in magnitude of our base case scenario.

to recover from 4Q onward, driven by rising

for AI servers have been rising since 2H26

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 6

Hon Precision (7769.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
6
Hon Precision
Company description
Hon Precision is a leading Taiwanese manufacturer specializing in IC
semiconductor automation equipment. Headquartered in Taichung City,
Taiwan, it is most known globally for providing high-precision IC test
handlers, temperature control solutions, and complete automated integrated
solutions for major OSAT and IDM customers.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Hon Precision as we view it sitting in a structurally

protected position at the heart of the AI/HPC back-end test cycle, with
growth visibility extending well into the next few years — the company holds
>90% share of the AI/HPC FT handler market and 60-70% of the ASIC SLT
market. We forecast a solid strong sales revenue CAGR in 2025-28E with
gross margin continuing to expand. In our view, the binding constraint on Hon
Precision's near-term trajectory is now capacity, rather than demand —
placing the company in the early phase of a multi-year structural growth and

margin-expansion cycle.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$10,000 is based on a target PER multiple of 31x applied
to the average of our 2027E/28E EPS estimates. Our PER target of 31x is set at
0.5-std above its 1-year PER average, justified by its high share of the AI/HPC
testing handler market, which we estimate could support an earnings CAGR
of > 75% in 2025-28, higher than its 3-year CAGR at c40% in 2022-25. Our
estimated > 75% earning CAGR is also higher than the +40% average

(Bloomberg consensus) of its global testing equipment peers. As its peers
have been trading at  average  35x PER on 2027 /28 consensus EPS in the past
1 year, we believe Hon Precision deserves a further re-rating to our target
PER.
Risks
Our quantitative risk rating system, which looks at historical share price
volatility, rates Hon Precision High Risk. However, we are not assigning a High
Risk rating as we forecast an earnings CAGR of > 75%, significantly stronger

than the growth outlook for its global peers. Also, we note its dominant
position in AI/HPC handler market and expanding market TAM opportunity,
which support our constructive view over its growth outlook.
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target
price: 1) Global AI demand slowdown  driving weaker capacity build for
semiconductor equipment; 2) unexpected share losses at its major AI
projects; 3) slower-than-expected capacity ramp at its new facilities, or the

company taking a long time to find/secure new sites; 4) slower-than-
expected development of its CPO business; 5) shorter-than-expected testing
time leading to lower demand for its products; 6) weak adoption of high-

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

Document 191: 70GW“电力缺口”！美国电网、民众和AI巨头，总得有一方“让步”，天量资本开支“无法落地”

Source PDF: all report pdf/70GW“电力缺口”！美国电网、民众和AI巨头，总得有一方“让步”，天量资本开支“无法落地”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

摩根⼠丹利测算显⽰，即便穷尽核能、燃料电池等所有解决⽅案，净缺⼝仍达 43GW 。更
棘⼿的是，燃⽓轮机价格已飙升⾄三倍，⼤型变压器交货期⻓达四年，劳动⼒短缺将 2030
年 IT 容量部署上限锁死在 142GW 。与此同时， 2023 年已有逾 1560 亿美元数据中⼼项⽬被
取消或延期， 70% 美国⼈反对在社区建设数据中⼼，政策阻⼒正将天量 AI 资本开⽀推向搁
浅边缘。
摘要：
⼈⼯智能的爆发式增⻓正与美国电⽹的物理极限发⽣激烈碰撞，由此产⽣的巨⼤电⼒缺⼝，正
对全球科技巨头的基础设施扩张计划构成实质性威胁。需求与供给之间的严重错配意味着，市
场预期的天量 AI 资本开⽀正⾯临 “ ⽆法落地 ” 的严峻⻛险，⽽电⽹、⺠众与科技巨头之间的博弈
已进⼊⽩热化阶段。
据摩根⼠丹利分析师 Stephen Byrd 的最新测算，美国数据中⼼开发商在 2028 年前⾯临⾼达
70GW 的电⼒缺⼝。即使穷尽所有潜在的解决⽅案，包括核能、燃料电池以及将部分需求转移⾄
亚洲，净电⼒缺⼝依然⾼达 43GW。这⼀数字在短短⼀年内激增了超过两倍，凸显了物理基础设

施对 AI 产业发展的硬性制约。
这⼀供需失衡正在迅速重塑市场定价与资本流向。⼀⽅⾯，能够提供 “ 电⼒交付时效 ” （ time to
power ）解决⽅案的设备供应商正获得极⾼的溢价，燃⽓轮机等核⼼设备价格已飙升两倍；另⼀
⽅⾯，随着电⼒成本上升和地⽅反对声浪加剧，信贷市场对 AI 相关债务的态度正变得挑剔。投资
者愈发担忧，部分庞⼤的数据中⼼建设计划最终将被迫延期或取消。
在电⼒成本飙升的背景下，美国⺠众对新建数据中⼼的反对情绪⽇益⾼涨，多州政府已开始实
施项⽬审批冻结。市场正⾯临⼀个冷酷的现实：要么科技巨头被迫削减其宏⼤的产能规划，要
么在获取电⼒的过程中承担前所未有的延期与成本代价。
尽管美国能源信息署（ EIA ）的最新数据显⽰，美国电⽹在 9 ⽉份实现了⼗年来最强劲的产能增
⻓，但这掩盖了深层次的结构性危机。新增产能的类型、地理位置和并⽹时间，完全⽆法匹配
数据中⼼全天候运转的严苛需求。整个⾏业正陷⼊由设备短缺、劳动⼒断层和政策阻⼒交织⽽
成的多重瓶颈之中。
70GW 缺⼝：最重要的约束仍是电⼒

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

摩根⼠丹利的 AlphaWise 调查清晰揭⽰了这场危机的核⼼症结所在。
调查显⽰， " 电⼒接⼊速度 " 是全球数据中⼼运营商反映的⾸要瓶颈，占⽐ 27% ；在美国，这⼀⽐
例⾼达 45% ，远超其他任何国家。 " 已供电⼟地的可⽤性 " 排名第⼆，美国同样以 26% 居全球⾸
位。
摩根⼠丹利的测算框架如下：  美国数据中⼼开发商到 2028 年合计需要 116GW 电⼒。在建数据中
⼼（含独⽴供电项⽬）覆盖 25GW ，可⽤电⽹接⼊覆盖 21GW ，由此产⽣ 70GW ⽑缺⼝。加计 " 快
速供电 " 解决⽅案 —— 合适的核电站选址（ 2GW ）、已公告的涡轮机和发动机（ 20GW ）、 Bloom
燃料电池（ 5GW ）、美国需求向亚洲溢出（ 5GW ） —— 净缺⼝仍达 38GW ，约为所需电⼒总量的
33% 。

### Page 3

摩根⼠丹利的美国数据中⼼团队以电⼒公司⼝径重新表述： 2028 年需求 180GW 对阵供给
137GW ， 43GW 的缺⼝较当前的 15GW 近乎扩⼤三倍。
需求激增与 “ ⽆效 ” 供给：电⽹结构的错配
华尔街投⾏正以前所未有的幅度上调电⼒需求预期。
Bernstein 分析师 Sunaina Ocalan 将其对 2030 年前美国电⼒需求的复合年增⻓率（ CAGR ）预测
从 3% 上调⾄ 4%。

### Page 4

该机构预计， 2025 年⾄ 2030 年间，美国将新增约 1000 太⽡时（ TWh ）的电⼒需求，其中超过⼀
半由数据中⼼贡献。
⾼盛的模型同样显⽰，到 2030 年，美国数据中⼼的电⼒需求将从 2025 年的 39GW 攀升⾄
108GW 。

### Page 5

然⽽，供给端的结构性错配令这⼀需求增⻓显得岌岌可危。⾼盛的数据显⽰， 9 ⽉份美国电⽹净
增 5.6GW 发电能⼒，但主要由太阳能（ 3.0GW ）和电池储能（ 1.6GW ）构成。对于需要 24 ⼩时
不间断运⾏的数据中⼼⽽⾔，太阳能的容量系数仅约为 25% ，⽆法提供稳定的基载电⼒。
能够满⾜这⼀需求的天然⽓发电产能建设则严重滞后。9 ⽉份，作为基载主⼒的联合循环燃⽓电
⼚新增产能为零。⾼盛指出，天然⽓发电项⽬的集中交付期要到 2028 年⾄ 2030 年才会到来，这
恰好错过了摩根⼠丹利预测的电⼒缺⼝⾼峰期。这种时间上的错位意味着，在未来⼏年内，电
⽹根本⽆法提供⾜够且合适的电⼒来⽀撑 AI 的扩张。
供应链与劳动⼒的双重枷锁
即使资⾦充裕，物理世界的供应链和劳动⼒短缺也构成了难以逾越的障碍。
在设备端，摩根⼠丹利指出， GE Vernova 的燃⽓轮机订单已排⾄ 2029 年，其价格已从 2020 年代
初的每千⽡ 1000 美元飙升⾄ 3000 美元。⼤型电⼒变压器的交货期⻓达 3 ⾄ 4 年，开关设备也需要

1 ⾄ 2 年。设备成本的全⾯上涨导致数据中⼼的建设成本⼤幅攀升，预计到本年代末，每 GW 的建
设成本将从 2022/23 年的 300 亿美元增⾄ 500 亿⾄ 600 亿美元。
⽐设备更棘⼿的是⼈⼒资源。⾼盛估计，到 2030 年，美国电⼒⾏业将需要超过 50 万名新⼯⼈。
即使在最乐观的学徒培训假设下，输配电领域到 2030 年仍将⾯临 78000 ⼈的劳动⼒缺⼝。
Bernstein 分析师 Chad Dillard 基于现有电⼯、管道⼯和暖通空调技术⼈员的物理建设能⼒建⽴的
模型显⽰，劳动⼒短缺可能成为最坚硬的制约因素，将 2030 年的 IT 容量部署上限锁定在
142GW ，远低于市场的乐观预期。

### Page 6

巨头的⾃救与 “ 电表后 ” 发电的兴起
⾯对电⽹的迟缓，科技巨头正在采取绕过传统电⽹的⾃救措施。⾼盛预计，到 2030 年，独⽴供
电（ Behind-the-Meter ， BTM ，即不经过公共电⽹直接为⽤⼾供电）将为 25% 的数据中⼼提供动
⼒。摩根⼠丹利则更为激进，预测到 2030 年美国 BTM 需求将达到 47GW 。
摩根⼠丹利基准情景假设 2028 年前将新增约 20GW 的 BTM 燃⽓涡轮机和发动机（区间 6 ⾄
38GW ），叠加约 6GW 在建装机及 6GW Bloom 燃料电池。其美国数据中⼼团队追踪到 2026 ⾄
2028 年制造商订单达 25 ⾄ 30GW ，并预计开发商从 2028 年起每年可新增 15GW 以上 BTM 产能。
但即便是最乐观的 " 快速供电 " 组合⽅案，仍将留下 17GW 的净缺⼝（基准情景下为 38GW ）。
核能成为了另⼀个备受追捧的选项。⾕歌、亚⻢逊等巨头近期频繁与 Constellation 等核电运营商
达成协议，试图通过重启现有反应堆或提升现有产能来获取电⼒。然⽽，远⽔难解近渴。摩根

⼠丹利指出，新建⼤型核电站的交货期⻓达约 15 年，⽽⼩型模块化反应堆（ SMR ）的商业化部
署要到 2030 年代中期才能实现，完全⽆法填补 2028 年的缺⼝。
在这⼀过程中，科技巨头对电价⼏乎不敏感。摩根⼠丹利的分析显⽰，即使电价上涨 17% （从
120 美元 / 兆⽡时升⾄ 140 美元 / 兆⽡时），⼀个 100MW 的 AI 数据中⼼的股本回报率（ ROE ）仅会
下降约 60 个基点，依然能保持在 30% 以上。这种对价格的不敏感，正将压⼒转嫁给普通纳税⼈
和电⽹系统。

### Page 7

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
⺠众反弹与政策阻⼒：谁来买单？
科技巨头愿意⽀付⾼昂的电价，但普通⺠众和选⺠却不愿为此买单。摩根⼠丹利估计，今年全
球电价上涨了约 20% 。在 PJM （美国最⼤的区域电⽹运营商）的最近四次容量拍卖中，数据中
⼼占据了 46% 的成本。
电费的飙升引发了强烈的政治反弹。盖洛普的最新⺠调显⽰，70% 的美国⼈反对在当地社区建
设数据中⼼，且这种反对跨越了党派界限。各州政府已开始采取⾏动：德克萨斯州在 9 ⽉份下令
暂停新建数据中⼼，并对 ERCOT （德州电⽹）互连流程中的所有项⽬展开审计；宾⼣法尼亚州
取消了快速审批通道；纽约州则暂停了 50MW 以上数据中⼼的环境许可。
政策阻⼒直接导致了资本开⽀的停滞。摩根⼠丹利的数据显⽰， 2023 年有约 1560 亿美元的数据
中⼼项⽬被取消或延期， 2024 年第⼀季度这⼀数字达到 1300 亿美元。随着 AI 相关债务发⾏量在

今年达到 4710 亿美元，信贷市场正变得愈发谨慎。
如果物理电⼒⽆法到位，天量资本开⽀计划⾯临被迫搁置的⻛险。在 2030 年数据中⼼预计将消
耗美国七分之⼀电⼒的背景下，电⽹、⺠众与 AI 巨头之间，注定将有⼀⽅必须做出妥协。

### Page 8

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick_cd366e43.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

(012450) 
한화에어로스페이스 
2026.10.30

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 1,660,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 760,000원(10/29)

 3Q26 연결 매출액은 6조 943억원(-6.0%yoy), 영업이익은 4,520억원(-
46.2%yoy, OPM 7.4%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센 1조 1,614억원을 하회.  
 지상방산의 손익이 크게 악화. 영업이익은 217억원(OPM 1.7%) 기록. 주요 
납품이 4분기에 집중된 가운데 일회성 비용 약 500억원(대전사업장 복구·
정상화 약 300억원, 국내 사업 충당금 약 160억원)과 환율 영향이 겹침.   
 다만 내용을 살펴보면 수출 사업의 수익성 훼손은 크지 않았다는 판단임.  
 일회성 제외 영업이익은 약 717억원(OPM 5.8%)이며, 일회성 비용은 대전
사업장이 국내 물량을 전담한다는 점에서 전액 국내 귀속으로 추정. 내수 
OPM을 일회성 제외 -5% 내외로 가정하면, 내수 영업이익은 약 -826억원
(일회성 포함 OPM -12.7%), 수출은 1,043억원(OPM 17.6%)으로 추정됨. 
 1Q26 대비로는 국내 마진 하락이, 2Q26 대비로는 수출 물량 감소가 가장 
크게 작용(도표 참고). 국내는 저마진 사업 매출이 늘고 고마진 반복 양산 물
량이 줄며 일시적으로 믹스가 악화. 수출은 폴란드 등 주요 인도가 정체되어 
매출이 감소한 가운데 저수익 사업 중심으로 믹스가 악화된 영향. 
 4Q26부터 회복 구간 진입 전망. 폴란드 K9 2차 실행계약 물량의 현지 인
도가 본격화되며, 회사는 2026년 폴란드 K9 30문 이상, 천무 발사대 60대 
이상(*연초 40대에서 상향) 인도를 계획. 수출입 데이터상으로도 폴란드·루
마니아향 출하가 대규모로 진행 중이며 폴란드향 부속류 수출도 정상적으로 
이어지는 것으로 확인. 일회성 소멸과 고마진 수출 집중으로 수출 OPM은 
30% 내외로 회복할 것. 다만 국내는 정상 가동(2027년 상반기 목표) 전까
지 한 자릿수 초반 마진에 머물 것으로 전망. 
 중장기 수익성 우려도 크지 않다는 판단. 2027~2029년 매출 연 15% 성
장 가이던스는 유지됐고, 내년 폴란드 K9·천무 인도 물량은 모두 올해 대비 
증가할 계획. 폴란드 사업 이후 높아진 수출 마진 레벨은 후속 사업에서도 
유지될 전망. 수주는 중동 지정학 불안으로 지연되고 있으나 대공체계 등 문
의가 이어지고 있어, 2027년부터 중동이 수주의 중심이 될 것으로 기대. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 166만원을 유지함. 3Q26 지상방산 
쇼크는 국내 적자와 수출 물량 공백이 겹친 일시적 결과로, 수출 경쟁력 훼
손으로 보기 어려움. 실적 충격에 따른 주가 조정은 분할 매수 기회로 판단.  
 한화에어로스페이스의 주가는 4월 7일 사상 최고가 153.7만원에서 10월 
29일 76.0만원으로 -51% 조정 받은 상황. 12MF P/E 기준 14.3x, 27E 
P/E는 13.9x로 밸류에이션 관점에서도 매력적인 구간 진입. 그동안 3분기 
실적 우려 등으로 부진했다면, 이제는 실적 바텀아웃과 대형 수주 랠리 속 
다시 한 번 반등을 기록할 차례. 동사를 방산업종 Top-pick으로 유지함.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

### Page 1

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 건설/원전 | 2026. 10. 30  
숫자로 증명하는 중

3Q26 Review: 에너빌부문 믹스개선, 두산밥캣 호실적 
두산에너빌리티 3분기 매출액은 4조 4,494억원으로 전년동기대비 14.7% 증가, 컨센
서스에 부합했다. 영업이익은 2,612억원으로 전년동기대비 90.6% 증가, 컨센서스 영
업이익 2,464억원 대비 6.0% 상회하는 호실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티부문의 원자
력, 가스터빈 매출 증가에 따른 믹스 개선 효과와 두산밥캣의 가격정책 효과에 힘입은 
마진 개선으로 안정적인 실적 기조가 이어지고 있어 긍정적이다. 분기 환평가손실로 
인해 순이익은 여타 수출기업과 유사하게 컨센서스 대비 하회했다. 에너빌부문의 수주
는 누계 10조원으로 목표대비 75%를 채웠으며, 수주 대기중인 안건들을 고려할 때 
당분간 수주 업사이클이 몇년간 지속될 것임이 분명한 시점이다. 
기회가 아닌 경쟁력, 모멘텀이 아닌 수주  
두산에너빌리티에 대해 투자의견 Buy 유지하나, 목표주가를 95,000원으로 하향했다. 
목표 EBITDA는 2035년 EBITDA에 WACC 8%를 적용, 목표 EV/EBITDA는 Global 
Peers인 GE Vernova의 2026년 EV/EBITDA 멀티플에서 자회사 실적 변동성을 고
려해 10% 할인 적용했다. 호실적과 수주 증가에도 GEV의 멀티플 감소와 순차입금 
증가로 인한 NAV 감소를 밸류에이션에 반영한 결과이다. 전일 주가는 한수원의 체코 
원전 시공사 협상 결렬로 인해 7.65% 하락 마감했다. 시공 수주는 아쉽지만 두산에너
빌리티의 체코 원전 기자재 납품에는 전혀 영향이 없으며, 글로벌 전력 수요 증가로 경
제적해자인 대형 가스 터빈 수급 쇼티지는 당분간 지속될 전망임을 고려할 때 전일 주
가는 낙폭 과대로 판단한다. 1) 두산에너빌리티의 가스터빈은 이미 2031년 출하 물량
에 대한 예약 주문이 마무리되고 있으며, 2) 또한 AP1000의 우량한 수주 파이프라인
과, 3) 뉴스케일파워, 테라파워, 롤스로이스, X Energy 등의 SMR 제작을 위한 협업 역
시 지속되고 있다는 점에 주목한다. 전일 기사를 통해 뉴스케일파워 SMR 1호 제작 계
약을 체결했다는 소식을 전했으며, 이제 에너지 트렌지션은 기회가 아닌 회사의 경쟁
력으로, 가스터빈과 원전은 모멘텀이 아니라 수주로 입증되고 있다.  
 Analyst 김세련 
sally.kim@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

(26.10.02)

제약업종

◼ 3Q26 컨센서스 부합, 4Q26 성장폭 확대 전망 
◼ 5공장 Ramp-up 지속, 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용)

3Q26 Preview: 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 전망 
3Q26 매출액 1조 3,380억원(+6.4% yoy, +1.3% qoq), 영업이익 5,890억원(-
7.0% yoy, +0.4% qoq, OPM 44.0%)으로 컨센서스에 부합하는 실적을 전
망함. 3분기 평균 원/달러 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락하고, 지난 5월 
파업에 따른 생산 차질 영향이 반영되나, 5공장 PPQ 물량의 매출 인식이 
시작되며 yoy, qoq 외형 성장은 이어질 것으로 예상함.  
파업 당시 생산이 지연된 물량은 3Q 중 추가 생산되어 4Q26부터 매출에 반
영될 전망임. 5공장은 다수 프로젝트의 PPQ가 진행 중이며, Ramp-up에 
따라 매출 기여도 점진적으로 확대될 것으로 예상함. 이에 4Q26에는 파
업 이연 물량 반영과 5공장 매출 확대에 따라 실적 성장폭이 확대될 전
망임. 2026년 연간 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 5조 5,310억원(+21.4% 
yoy), 2조 4,780억원(+19.8% yoy, OPM 44.8%)으로 전망함.

신규 수주 회복 기대, 중장기 성장 기반은 확대 중 
관세 관련 불확실성으로 둔화됐던 고객사와의 수주 논의가 재개되고 있음. 
10월 2일 유럽 소재 제약사와 935억원 규모의 CMO 계약을 추가 체결
했으며, 4Q26 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목함.  
PolyPeptide 인수는 연내 거래 종결을 목표로 진행 중이며, 인수 완료 
시 펩타이드 분야로 CDMO 포트폴리오가 확대될 전망임. 6 공장도 연내 
착공을 목표로 준비 중임. 
한편, 노사 리스크는 단기 불확실성 요인임. 노조는 10/26부터 3주간 2
차 총파업을 예고함. 1차 대비 예정된 파업 기간이 길어진 만큼 실제 파
업 진행 시 생산 차질 규모가 주요 변수임. 다만 파업 개시 전까지 협상 
가능성이 열려 있어, 향후 노사 협상 진행 경과를 확인할 필요가 있음.

산업 투자의견 
- Overweight(비중확대) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 초과 상승 예상 
- Neutral(중립) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률과 유사한 수준 예상 
- Underweight(비중축소) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 하회 예상

기업 투자의견 
- Buy(매수)  
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 상승 예상 
- Marketperform(시장수익률) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 -10%p~10%p 주가 변동 예상 
- Underperform(시장수익률 하회) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 하락 예상

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +68.24 điểm tương ứng +4.00%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Thiết bị điện, Khách sạn - Nhà hàng và Thiết bị dịch vụ y tế là các nhóm ngành có 
mức tăng rõ rệt nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần 
trước với giá trị -966 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh duy 
trì mua ròng ở mức 137 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,335 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -3,049 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
1,815 hợp đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,000 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam hồi phục trở lại sau bốn tuần giảm liên tiếp, khi VN-Index bật tăng mạnh 
từ vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1.700 điểm với thanh khoản cải thiện. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh kéo dài 
khiến mặt bằng định giá về vùng hấp dẫn, lực cầu bắt đáy đã nhập cuộc quyết liệt hơn, 
với phiên 21/10 tăng gần 47 điểm — một trong những phiên tăng mạnh nhất nhiều tháng 
gần đây. Điểm tích cực và đáng chú ý nhất là khối ngoại đã xuất hiện hai phiên đảo chiều 
mua ròng trở lại sau khoảng 20 phiên bán ròng liên tiếp, tập trung vào nhóm Bluechip 
như VIC, TCB, VHM, MSN — tín hiệu đầu tiên cho thấy áp lực rút vốn ngoại có thể đang dần 
hạ nhiệt. Thông tin tích cực tác động lên thị trường đến từ các chỉ đạo của NHNN và Chính 
phủ về việc hạ mặt bằng lãi suất để hỗ trợ tăng trưởng kinh tế. Bên cạnh đó, mùa báo cáo 
KQKD quý 3 khả quan đang diễn ra cũng là yếu tố hỗ trợ xu hướng thị trường chung.

19/10/2026

Nguồn: V_KBSource

Triển vọng kinh tế 4Q2026 
Giữ nhịp tăng trưởng, vững vàng về itch

Một số dự báo chính của KBSV về kinh tế Việt Nam trong năm 2026:

1) Tăng trưởng GDP năm 2026 được dự báo sẽ đạt 9.0%, với động lực chính 
đến từ định hướng đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư công của Chính phủ, dòng 
vốn FDI duy trì tích cực và hoạt động sản xuất công nghiệp phục hồi theo 
xuất khẩu. Về rủi ro, chúng tôi cho rằng Việt Nam sẽ tiếp tục đối mặt với 
một số yếu tố bất định từ bên ngoài, bao gồm: rủi ro xung đột Mỹ – Iran 
kéo dài hơn dự kiến, xu hướng tăng của mặt bằng lãi suất toàn cầu gây gia 
tăng áp lực tài chính và kéo giảm tổng cầu, cùng nguy cơ vỡ bong bóng AI.

2) Chúng tôi nâng dự báo lạm phát bình quân năm 2026 của Việt Nam lên 
mức 4.7% YoY, cao hơn mục tiêu kiểm soát lạm phát của Chính phủ, dựa 
trên kịch bản dự báo thận trọng hơn về diễn biến xung đột địa chính trị và 
giá năng lượng thế giới trong ngắn hạn.

3) Mặt bằng lãi suất trong quý IV được dự báo sẽ ổn định quanh vùng hiện 
tại và không xuất hiện thêm nhịp tăng đột biến, nhờ dòng tiền cải thiện 
từ giải ngân đầu tư công và kỳ vọng có thêm các biện pháp hỗ trợ thanh 
khoản trung dài hạn từ phía Chính phủ và NHNN.

4) Tỷ giá USD/VND trong quý cuối năm có thể chịu một số sức ép do Fed dự 
báo còn thêm một lần tăng lãi suất nữa trong năm. Dù vậy, chúng tôi cho 
rằng tỷ giá USD/VND khó tăng mạnh mà chỉ quay lại mức đầu năm, dựa 
trên kỳ vọng chỉ số DXY không biến động mạnh, nguồn cung ngoại tệ cải 
thiện (FDI giải ngân, kiều hối, nâng hạng TTCK) và chênh lệch lãi suất 
VND–USD duy trì trạng thái dương.

Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

### Page 4

21/10/2026

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025. 
— PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ. 
Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

Cam  Khả năng giảm điểm vừa phải Bán từng phần 
Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến xanh thân dài trên khung tuần và đáng chú ý là cho phản ứng 
bật tăng ngay tại cạnh dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn, cho thấy tín hiệu 
tích cực đã quay trở lại với xu hướng ngắn hạn. Trên khung ngày, sau 2 phiên điều chỉnh, 
rung lắc vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng đảo chiều tăng điểm khá dốc, với 3 cây nến xanh liên 
tiếp với thanh khoản được tăng cường, củng cố thêm tín hiệu xác nhận cho đà hồi phục. 
Sau nhịp bật tăng mạnh từ đáy, VNINDEX được dự báo sẽ sớm gặp phải áp lực điều chỉnh 
khi quay lên điểm giữa của dải đi ngang theo mẫu hình tam giác, tương ứng vùng kháng cự 
kế tiếp tại quanh 1810. Tuy nhiên, xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn có phần nghiêng về chiều tăng, 
với tín hiệu xác nhận đáy của một số mã cổ phiếu sau quá trình sụt giảm kéo dài.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

Kháng cự gần: 1810 (+-10)

Document 319: Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation

Source PDF: all report pdf/Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Downgrade of global 
equities to a Core allocation  
Summary 
Event: We are downgrading global equities from Overweight 
to a Neutral/Core allocation. We continue to maintain a 
Neutral allocation to fixed income, Overweight on Gold and 
Underweight on Cash. There are more details in our Global 
Market Outlook publication on 30 October 2026. 
What does the equity downgrade mean?  
This downgrade implies a slightly lower allocation to global 
equities within a diversified portfolio. For our CIO Foundation 
Balanced portfolio, this would imply an equity allocation of 
52.5%, down from 53 .2%. We continue to expect positive 
returns for global equities  in the coming 6 -12 months , 
though equity investors may need to recalibrate expectations 
lower, compared with the 15 -20% annualised gains 
experienced in the last few years. Our capital market 
assumptions still suggest a mid -to-high single digit annual 
returns over the next few years, consistent with a transition 
to a mid -cycle bull market . Consensus estimates point to 
16% earnings growth for global equities in 2027, which 
should overcome various headwinds to deliver positive 
returns for equities over the next 12 months. Investors should 
thus, continue to maintain exposure to equities  within a 
diversified portfolio. 
In addition, we see a changing playbook  to prepare for 
higher volatility, more active management and greater 
diversification, as discussed below. 
What does it mean for regional equities 
positioning? 
Within a global equities portfolio, we remain Overweight US 
and Asia ex -Japan (AxJ) equities , which are expected to 
deliver the highest earnings growth in 2027 amongst the 
major regions. This growth is led by the technology sector, 
which carries the largest weight in both regions. Within the 
diversified equities portfolio, we maintain Core allocations to

53.2% for our CIO Foundation Balanced portfolio

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

Euro area equities, which offer lower valuations, and Japan 
equities, which is backed by a reflating economy. We are 
Underweight UK equities, where the sector composition 
offers lower growth prospects in 2027. Within the AxJ region, 
we are Overweight Taiwan equities, with attractive structural 
growth driven by its semiconductor industry. We are also 
Overweight India equities, which offers diversified non-tech 
growth drivers. Taiwan and India equities both generate 
attractive returns on equity. We maintain Core allocations to 
China equities, with low valuations, and Korea equities, with 
explosive earnings growth in its semiconductors industry, but 
volatile price movements. We are Underweight ASEAN, 
which offers relatively lower earnings growth. 
What are the implications for equity sectors? 
We are Overweight these sectors globally: US 
information technology, US healthcare, US financials, US 
materials, Europe ex -UK financials, and Japan 
financials. 
A framework we use is to break the market into four key 
sector building blocks. Tech is the largest block, 
encompassing the Information Technology, Communication 
Services and Consumer Discretionary sectors. Put 
differently, the segment spans five major industries: 
semiconductors, hardware, internet, software, and IT 
services. Within the US market, we expect this segment to 
deliver c.30% earnings growth in 2027, extending the strong 
momentum seen in 2026. 
Financials and Real Estate  are the next building block, 
comprising these two sectors. We expect this segment to 
continue delivering low -teens earnings growth in 2027, 
supported by resilient economic activity and healthy credit 
trends. 
Resources include companies across four sectors: 
Industrials, Materials, Energy, and Utilities . We expect the 
segment to generate 10 -15% earnings growth in 2027, 
although Energy is likely to remain the largest drag on overall 
growth due to tougher comparisons and softer energy price 
assumptions for 2027. 
Healthcare & Others  consists primarily of Healthcare and 
Consumer Staples. We expect mid-teen earnings growth for 
the segment in 2027, with a rebound in healthcare earnings 
representing a potential upside surprise. 
Our Overweight sector exposures are drawn from each of the 
four key blocks above. Unlike the last few years , with 
concentrated gains from Tech providing market leadership, 
we see our downgrade in global equities also reflecting a 
broadening in market leadership towards each of the other 
sector blocks apart from Tech.

As structural themes such as AI transition from the rapid 
buildout phase towards monetisation, diversified exposure 
across sectors and regions should become increasingly 
important. 
What are the implications for equity 
opportunistic ideas? 
We have opportunistic ideas on Global High -Dividend 
equities, US healthcare, Euro area banks , MSCI 
Switzerland and Japan banks. The selection represents a 
more defensive stance  currently, with an average beta of 
below 1 relative to global equities (MSCI AC World), but 
attractive drivers nonetheless, for each of the ideas.  
Global High-Dividend offers higher-income equity allocation 
with historically lower volatility than global equities. US 
healthcare is supported by wider adoption of obesity 
treatments, advance s in oncology a nd innovative medical 
devices, that support earnings growth. Euro area banks  
continue to see steady earnings growth adding to their 
excess capital, which is being returned to shareholders via 
attractive dividend and share buyback yields. Japan banks 
continue to enjoy net margin expansion from BoJ policy 
normalisation, as well as corporate governance 
improvements. MSCI Switzerland is a newly initiated idea for 
investors seeking defensive exposure. H ealthcare and 
consumer staples account s for more than half the index 
weight, providing exposure to relatively resilient demand that 
is more insulated from economic cycles and AI trends. It also 
has a sizeable financial sector supported by steady wealth 
management growth and well-capitalised insurers with 
disciplined underwriting and resilient premium income. 
We see the case for more active management  in a mid -
cycle transition, as higher market dispersion should create 
greater opportunities for active sector and stock selection, 
particularly as leadership within equities evolves. 
What are the implications of higher equity 
market volatility?  
A more uncertain political backdrop and p otentially maturing 
growth rates in certain market segments may contribute to 
more frequent bouts of market volatility. Higher volatility 
presents an opportunity to use structured strategies to gain 
exposure to equities. Such strategies may be used to 
generate income or to gain exposure at a discounted entry 
price. Investors can also consider strategies that are 
structured to return principal or partial principal at maturity. 
What alternative strategies are suited to a 
regime of moderating equity market returns?  
Investors can consider alternative strategies such as hedge  
fund strategies or private markets, for those with the capacity

to accept some illiquidity.  
Equity long/short hedge fund  strategies would retain growth 
participation with less market exposure. Fund managers buy 
companies they expect to outperform and short those they 
expect to underperform, seeking to earn the difference 
between the two , and benefit even as total equity market 
returns are moderating. 
Within private markets , private equity provides  a means to 
higher returns with the effects of an illiquidity premium. 
Meanwhile, i nfrastructure offers  differentiated exposure to 
structural growth opportunities, where c ontracted long-term 
revenues reduce dependence on equity-market sentiment. 
What are the implications on fixed income 
and FX markets? 
Bonds: This year’s surge in bond yields means bonds now 
offer a hedge against any equity market weakness. We 
believe the 3 -7-year part of the US bond yield curve offers 
the best hedge against any equity sell -off caused by AI -
related growth worries. This part of the bond curve has priced 
in most Fed rate hike expectations, which could reverse if 
growth concerns return. Meanwhile, we favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a hedge against any 
fuel price-led inflation scare.  
Credit: For corporate bonds, adopting a defensive stance in 
a volatile environment typically involves: i) increasing credit 
quality, favouring higher-rated issuers; and ii) moving up the 
capital structure, by reducing exposure to subordinated debt 
and favouring secured debt. From a sector perspective, 
large, diversified banks, utilities, and quasi -sovereigns tend 
to be more defensive, particularly relative to cyclical sectors, 
such as metals and mining, exploration, and technology.         
FX: We favour bearish AUD/JPY as a tactical hedge against 
any equity market correction. Review of 21 past S&P500 
correction sessions (2010 -2025) shows AUD weakened 
against USD in 86% of cases, while JPY strengthened 
against USD in 67%, supporting our bearish AUD/JPY 
outlook during any equity market stress.

Document 380: Opportunities from the bond sell-off

Source PDF: all report pdf/Opportunities from the bond sell-off.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Weekly Market Views)
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 14
Status: ok

### Page 1

WS Global CIO Office 
2 October 2026

Opportunities from 
the bond sell-off

 The global bond sell-off is creating 
tactical and medium-term opportunities. 
In the US, September’s yield surge has 
improved the risk-reward in 10-year 
government bonds, presenting an 
attractive entry point for investors with 
a 6-12-month horizon. 
 We believe bond and money 
markets are overly hawkish about the 
Fed outlook, given disinflation is likely 
to return as the impact of oil prices and 
tariffs ease next year. 
 In equities, we stick to quality, 
especially sectors riding the AI-led 
earnings boom. The upcoming Q3 
earnings season is likely to show AI-
investments are powering sectors 
beyond the technology and 
communications services. We remain 
constructive on the semiconductor and 
memory segments.  
 We also expect the US Dollar to 
peak in the next few weeks along with 
short-to-medium-term US bond yields. 
This creates opportunities for AUD 
corporate bonds, Emerging Market

Editorial 
Opportunities from the bond sell-off  
Strategy summary: The global bond sell-off is creating tactical 
and medium-term opportunities. In the US, September’s yield 
surge has improved the risk -reward in 10 -year government 
bonds, presenting an attractive entry point for investors with a 
6-12-month horizon. We believe bond and money markets are 
overly hawkish about the Fed outlook, given disinflation is likely 
to return as impact of oil prices and tariffs ease next year. 
In equities, we stick to quality, especially sectors riding the AI -
led earnings boom. The upcoming Q3 earnings season is likely 
to show AI -investments are powering sectors beyond the 
technology and communications services, such as financials, 
materials and power and electrification. We remain constructive 
on the semiconductor and memory segments. We also expect 
the US Dollar to peak in the next few weeks along with short -
to-medium-term US bond yields. This creates opportunities for 
AUD corporate bonds, Emerging Market USD bonds and gold. 
Attractive risk -reward in US 10 -year government bonds.  
The US 10-year bond yield rose more than 50bps in September 
(biggest monthly rise since 2022) and 115bps this year, amid 
expectations of economic resilience, higher Fed rates, fuel -
driven inflation, AI-related capital demand and fiscal concerns. 
Valuations appear close to fair value, improving the 6-12-month 
risk-reward. Bond arithmetic suggests losses of less than 1% if 
the yield rises to 6%, vs. more than 10% gains if it falls to 4.5%. 
Resilient US economic activity, but softer -than-expected 
inflation. September data showed robust US employment 
(jobless claims, ADP) and core capital-goods orders. However, 
consumer confidence remains depressed, real disposable 
income is flat and households are drawing on savings to sustain 
spending. Against this divided backdrop, core PCE inflation - 
the Fed’s preferred gauge - was softer than expected at 0.2% 
m/m and 3.0% y/y. A revised method to measure PCE inflation 
also indicate that annual core PCE inflation was 35bps lower 
than previously reported, narrowing the gap with CPI inflation. 
Markets appear overly hawkish on the Fed. Money markets 
price about 85bps of tightening over the next 12 months. A 
strong labour market (September’s non -farm payrolls data is 
due tonight) may justify one or two further hikes, but aggressive 
tightening after the sharp rise in bond yields – which has 
already tightened financial conditions - could threaten financial 
stability. US small- and mid-cap equities have already fallen 8–
9% from recent peaks over the past couple of months, 
materially underperforming large caps, as energy and 
borrowing-cost concerns intensify. W hile some Fed speakers 
remain hawkish, we  agree with New York Fed President 
Williams’ that there is no urgency to tighten. We see little 
chance of an October Fed rate hike and expect inflation data 
for Sep-Nov determining whether the Fed moves in December. 
Stay with quality equities and AI beneficiaries.  US large 
caps have absorbed the rise in bond yields relatively well, 
supported by expectations of robust earnings growth led by AI-
related investment. We continue to favour quality companies 
and sectors benefiting from this AI cycle. Semiconductors and 
memory remain preferred exposures: structural AI demand 
should extend the memory upcycle, while long -term supply 
agreements provide better revenue visibility. The opportunity is 
also broadening beyond technology and communication 
services into financials, materials, power and electrification. 
Opportunities in AUD corporate bonds and gold.  The USD 
rebound alongside higher US yields offers a tactical opportunity 
to add higher -yielding Emerging Market USD and non -USD 
bonds. We remain positive on AUD corporate bonds, which 
offer a yield premium over comparable US credit. Our 
constructive medium-term AUD/USD view could add returns for 
USD-based investors over the next 6 –12 months. We would 
also use gold’s recent pullback to add exposure, supported by 
sustained Emerging Market central bank demand. We continue 
to view USD 4,000/oz as important technical support for gold.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

Top client questions 
  Do you expect more RBA rate hikes after its less hawkish 
guidance? What is your view on the AUD and AUD bonds? 
Our view: We expect the RBA to leave rates unchanged, but 
retain the option to hike again should inflation intensify. We 
maintain our medium-term constructive AUD view and maintain 
our opportunistically bullish AUD corporate bonds idea. 
Rationale: The RBA’s forward guidance remains data dependent, 
explicitly allowing further tightening if needed as inflation remains too 
high.  At 4.60%, Australia’s cash rate is currently 60–85bp above the 
Fed’s 3.75–4.00% range. Thus, even with our two additional 25bp s 
Fed hikes expectation, Australian rates would hold a modest positive 
rate differential. We expect the AUD to strengthen once the Fed 
signals that US rates are near their peak, which should allow resilient 
Australian fundamentals to reassert themselves. Key risks are 
persistently high US yields, weaker China growth or a sharper 
deterioration in global risk sentiment. 
We believe risk-reward has become more attractive for AUD bonds 
following the bear -flattening of the AUD yield curve. Markets are 
already pricing in one more RBA hike through end-2027, presenting 
an attractive opportunity to earn a yield even as increasingly 
restrictive financial conditions reinforc e disinflation over time. 
Meanwhile, the positive interest rate differential with the US supports 
the currency outlook, supporting the case for AUD bonds. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  What does the incoming US data tell us about the path for 
US 10-year bond yields from here? 
Our view: The risk -reward in US 10 -year yields has turned 
attractive following their move higher amid incoming US data. 
Rationale: Recent US data has indeed started to show softer-than-
expected inflation. However, market technicals are likely temporarily 
delaying a move lower in bond yields, with reports suggesting the  
rise in yields is creating forced selling amongst hedge funds and real 
estate investment trusts. Nevertheless, we do not expect this to be 
an extended selloff. We have initiated an opportunistic bullish idea 
on US 10-year government bonds this week and continue to believe 
this idea offers an attractive risk/reward on a 6-12-month horizon as 
yields become attractive enough to entice buyer flow to return. On a 
broader allocation basis, we retain our structural preference for 3-7-
year maturities in bonds. 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in the GBP ahead of the UK 
budget announcement next month? 
Our view: We maintain a medium-term constructive GBP view, 
but keep near-term exposure measured ahead of the 28 October 
Budget. We would view any pre -Budget weakness as an 
opportunity to gradually build GBP exposure. 
Rationale: The government’s decision to adjust the pension triple 
lock from 2030 is an early indication of greater willingness to tackle 
longer-term spending pressures, reinforcing Chancellor Healey’s 
commitment to fiscal discipline and meeting the fiscal rules. Markets 
are likely to scrutinise the Budget closely for evidence that this 
discipline extends to near -term tax and spending decisions. A 
credible Budget should help contain pressure on UK government 
bond yields and support GBP. Any fiscal slippage remains a key risk.  
Near term, we prefer measured GBP exposure as elevated US yields 
and repricing towards further Fed tightening could continue to 
support the USD. Over the medium term, we expect GBP to rebound 
as the Fed eventually signals the peak of its tightening cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  How would different US mid-term election outcome 
scenarios affect the outlook for US equities? 
Our view: We believe election outcomes will have a limited 
impact over the next 12 months. We remain positive on US 
equities driven by fundamental earnings growth.  
Rationale: Over the past 50 years, the ruling US President’s party 
has retained control of both chambers of Congress on only two 
occasions (1978 and 2002). All other elections result ed in an 
opposition sweep or a divided government.  
Based on the latest prediction markets, it appears that an opposition 
sweep (both the House and Senate flips from Republican to 
Democrats) is more likely than a divided government ( in which the 
House flips to Democrats , while the Senate remains under 
Republican control). A scenario where Republicans retain a majority 
is least likely per prediction markets. 
While mid -term elections may introduce near -term volatility, we 
caution against overreaction. Historically, the average return for the 
S&P 500 following an opposition sweep is 17% over the next 12 
months, but the average return following a divided government is not 
significantly worse, at 15%. We prefer to base our investment views 
on fundamental earnings growth, where investor attention should 
focus on the upcoming Q3 earnings season. 
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is the outlook for  the memory chip and broader 
semiconductors industry? 
Our view: We remain positive on semiconductors and memory 
chips. Structural AI demand should prolong the memory 
upcycle while long-term agreements improve revenue visibility. 
Rationale: Recent industry commentary points to tighter memory 
supply-demand conditions through 2028, reinforcing our view that AI 
demand is likely to extend the memory upcycle. Furthermore, adoption 
of long-term agreements improves revenue and earnings visibility. 
Despite debates around “Pacing the Frontier”, we have seen a flurry 
of new model releases, along with the introduction of new consumer 
AI agents capable of comparing prices online and making 
purchases. These are early days, but the path is open for a business-
to-agent (B2A) economy where businesses increasingly interact with 
AI assistants acting on customers’ behalf. As these applications are 
increasingly inference intensive, wider adoption should support 
demand for memory, with its share of global semiconductor 
revenues projected to rise from 30.7% in 2025 to 66.7% in 2027. 
Against this backdrop, we maintain our Opportunistic idea on Global 
Semiconductors. Given memory  chips’ cyclical nature, investors 
should avoid overconcentration in memory names and maintain a 
diversified exposure across Big Tech and semiconductors. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  Does a US bond yield stress test raise the risk to your 
constructive view on US equities? 
Our view: We have a target for the S&P 500 index of 8400 over 
the next 12 months. A scenario of a sustained rise in the US 10-
year government bond yield to 5.6% could lead to negative 
returns from the current S&P500 level (7,666). 
Rationale: Our base case 12-month target for the S&P 500 is 8,400, 
which assumes 2027 S&P 500 EPS of USD 420 and a 20x forward 
P/E multiple. A rise in bond yields would have an impact on equity 
valuations, as a higher discount rate reduces the present value of 
future earnings. W e stress -test our target under different 10 -year 
yield and earnings scenarios.  
A rise in bond yields would arguably be associated with a stronger 
growth environment and hence, even higher earnings. However, 
assuming unchanged EPS at our base case of USD 420, a 
(sustained) rise in the 10 -year bond yield to 5.6% would lead to 
negative returns from the current level  if not offset by a further 
improvement in earnings growth . However, in o ur base case  we 
expect the 10-year yield is more likely to fall from here. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist

Document 396: Riksbank to hike despite soft inflation and rallying krona

Source PDF: all report pdf/Riksbank to hike despite soft inflation and rallying krona.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

We expect a 25bp hike on 4 November by the Riksbank,
alongside a reiteration of the tightening bias. Inﬂation was
softer than expected in September, but upside risks remain for
prices, and a resilient economy allows policymakers to tighten
pre-emptively. We have revised our EUR/SEK year-end target to
11.00 after the recent rally
Francesco Pesole
We expect two rate hikes in Sweden in the next few months
The Riksbank announces policy on 4 November, and we are sticking

to our call for a 25bp rate hike to 2.0%. Market bets on a rate
increase next week have dwindled in the past week or so, moving
from 22-24bp to 15-18bp. While we don’t see a single clear trigger
for this partial dovish rethink, a soft inﬂation print in September
and SEK appreciation may have both played a role.
However, both in-meeting and oﬀ-meeting commentary by
Riksbank oﬃcials have been endorsing a move at this November

### Page 2

meeting, and the rise in energy prices oﬀsets the dovish inﬂation
and currency market inputs, in our view.
Don’t expect an explicit commitment to further increases, but the
tightening bias should remain in place. Our call remains that the
Riksbank will skip December and hike again to 2.25% on 3
February. We don’t currently expect any further rate increases
beyond that, but the risks are clearly on the hawkish side given the
persistence of energy price inﬂation.

Muted inﬂation in September, but goods
prices are picking up
Swedish inﬂation was slightly more subdued than expected in
September. CPIF came in at 1.5% year-on-year as expected, but
CPIF excluding energy was just 0.5%, 0.2ppt below the Riksbank’s
forecast. The softness was concentrated in food and services, with
an unusually large drop in car rental prices playing a role.
But the details were not entirely dovish. Goods inﬂation was
stronger and broad-based, with durable goods prices rising 1.1%

month-on-month and annual inﬂation turning positive for the ﬁrst
time in over a year. That ﬁts with earlier signals from producer
prices and suggests higher global price pressures are starting to
feed through.
We remain aligned with the Riksbank’s projections that CPIF
inﬂation should reach 2.5% and CPIF excluding energy 1.0% in
December. Further on, the outlook remains for further acceleration
in core measures, with CPIF excluding energy set to pass 2.0% by

the summer.
Growth proved resilient again in 3Q, with GDP rising 0.4% quarter-
on-quarter and 2.8% YoY.
Higher energy prices to tilt the balance
towards November
These inﬂation projections are only facing upside risks the longer
energy prices remain elevated. Alongside them, the Riksbank

### Page 3

signalled a split probability rates would be raised at either the
November or December meeting.
While the September inﬂation print and stronger krona (which we
address below as a topic) may argue for a pause, the rise in energy
prices is a bigger deciding factor in our view, and should tilt the
balance towards a rate hike already next week. In September, the
Brent crude price projection for end-2026 was $92/bbl, against the
current price of $118. European gas prices have also increased

c.35% since the September meeting. New projections are only
published in December, but we think the Riksbank will have to
acknowledge that higher energy prices increase the risk of more
persistent inﬂation.
On top of that, Riksbank oﬃcials have been generally hawkish-
leaning in their comments in the past month. Governor Erik
Thedeen said there are “strong reasons” to hike in the near term,
partly due to the resilient economy. Aino Bunge and Per Jansson

have both broadly endorsed that view in later comments.
Higher SEK, higher energy since last policy
meeting
Source: ING, Riksbank, Reﬁnitiv
Riksbank shouldn’t underestimate SEK
fragility if it holds

### Page 4

There’s no denying that the hawkish shift in September was partly
due to the Swedish krona’s weakness. Since that last policy
meeting, the krona has appreciated c.1.4% on a trade-weighted
basis, driven by a drop in EUR/SEK. That, in turn, has been a
consequence of a tightening in two-year swap rate diﬀerentials of
around 20bp, mirroring a stable outlook for Riksbank tightening
while ECB expectations were revised lower on the back of French-
related risk.

EUR/SEK was also trading 1-2% above our short-term fair value
estimates at the end of September. The subsequent rise in euro
idiosyncratic risk has pushed the pair into a modest 0.5-1%
undervaluation.
We don’t think that is enough to justify a dovish Riksbank surprise.
Despite the recent rebound, SEK remains 4% weaker on a trade-
weighted basis since the start of the year. More importantly, the
hawkish Riksbank stance is itself an endogenous driver of recent

SEK strength, with short-term rate diﬀerentials overwhelmingly
dominant for the currency.
A hold or dovish hike would trigger fresh SEK weakness. With the
energy story still negative and contingent euro idiosyncratic risk
potentially abating, SEK could return to late September levels again
without the Riksbank’s support.
Revising our EUR/SEK target lower
Our EUR/SEK year-end target has hovered around 11.10-11.15 for
the past couple of months. The build-up of substantial EUR-speciﬁc

risk makes a lower target an obvious adjustment. Markets are also
not fully pricing a Riksbank hike next week, with 17bp priced at the
time of writing, nor a February follow-up move, with 40bp priced,
which remains our baseline.
Still, EUR/SEK looks a bit too weak relative to fundamentals. That
points to a lingering risk premium and leaves room for upside if
some France-related risk abates into year-end (our baseline). At the
same time, the external backdrop remains challenging for SEK, with

oil prices still very elevated.

We are revising our year-end EUR/SEK target to 11.00, close to
current spot. Over the next week, however, we could see further

downside exploration, with a potential break below 10.90, as a
Riksbank hike may combine with some US-related risk around the
midterms to support SEK, both through the rates channel and
potential capital repatriation ﬂows.
In the longer run, we still expect a gradual decline to 10.70 over the
course of next year.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
2
EXECUTIVE SUMMARY
• The Westpac–DataX Card Tracker
Index* has edged down slightly since
late September, slipping 0.9pts to 160.6
in the week ended October 17. Despite
this, growth in activity continues to track
a robust pace with quarterly momentum

running at close to 2%qtr and annual
growth at 6%yr.
• Some of this reflects revisions. Initial
figures on card activity can see changes
in subsequent weeks due to information
lags. While these are usually small,
the cumulative impact can sometimes
be material. In this case, revisions have
lifted the index in late September by
2.5pts and the 4wk trend growth rate at
the time from 0.2% to 0.6%. We will be
back-testing regular revisions to assess
whether there is a systematic bias that

can be adjusted for in future.
• The revised profile shows quarterly
growth accelerating steadily since
June, posting a 1.9%qtr gain for the
September quarter and tracking a
2.1%qtr pace in the latest week. This is
back near the robust pace seen in late
2024 and early 2025.
• The monthly and weekly reads show
a softer 0.2%mth rise in August was
followed by a solid 0.8%mth gain in
September that has largely carried into
the first two weeks of October (the latest

observation tracking a 0.4%mth pace).
• The detail shows gains are not just
due to higher fuel prices with strong
growth in discretionary services and
international transactions over the last
quarter and steady growth in essential
services as well. To the extent that the
weekly and monthly data is showing
some renewed squeeze from higher fuel
prices, this is showing through most
clearly in discretionary goods, which
have seen activity decline over the last

six weeks, with discretionary services
still posting positive, albeit slower
growth. Quarterly growth in non-fuel
card activity is tracking around 1.4%qtr.
• Official September quarter figures on
inflation and household spending due
in the coming weeks should confirm the
spending momentum and shed more
light on the extent to which it is due to
prices or volumes.

running at close to 2%qtr ...”

are a timely guide to shifts in spending. See p 10 for a detailed explanation.

• Chart 2 shows how quarterly growth
in the Westpac–DataX Card Tracker

• Only some of this reflects a price-

and more recent surge in global energy

activity has moderated, but only slightly,

from 1.9%qtr in August to 1.8% in

more pronounced in monthly terms with

+0.2% gain in September and subdued
+0.1% monthly pace in the latest week
(c.f. total: –0.4%; +0.2% and +0.1%).

expected price rise of 1.1%qtr point to a
real, inflation-adjusted gain of 0.8%qtr,
in line with Westpac’s forecasts. ABS Q3

indicator due Nov 4 and GDP due Dec 2.

• Chart 4 shows quarterly growth in card

what looked to be a slowing in momentum
at the time. We will be investigating this

issue more closely in coming weeks to see
whether there is a systematic bias that
can be adjusted for in the future.

6. Card activity: retail segments and non-retail

### Page 6

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
WESTPAC ECONOMICS 6
• The Westpac Card Tracker draws on
a rich dataset that sheds light on many
aspects of consumer demand. In some
cases, it can also reveal some nuances
about the way spend is captured – in
official statistics, industry measures and
the card data itself. Vehicles provide a

particularly good example of this.
• Vehicle spend has long been a
bellwether for the wider consumer cycle.
As a large discretionary expenditure, it is
more sensitive to interest rate changes,
‘wealth effects’ and sentiment shifts,
particularly around job security. Given
this, ABS measures and industry figures
on vehicle sales have delivered material
upside surprises over the last year.
Vehicle purchases recorded a remarkable
10.3%qtr surge in the June quarter to be

up 7.3%yr with industry figures showing
vehicle sales holding around these levels
in the September quarter. Even allowing
‘extenuating circumstances’ driving
strong demand for EVs, the results seem
to fly in the face of wider interest rate,
cost-of-living and sentiment narratives.
• The card tracker data allows for a deeper
dive. Note that credit and debit cards are
not often used to purchase vehicles with
account transfers or financing typically

used for the main purchase instead.
• However, cards are used for up-front
payments. This shows through in
payment sizes, which are in the hundreds
of dollars (average $750) not the tens of
thousands. As such, card activity should
still give some guide, particularly around
sales volumes.
• Chart 8 shows how transactions have
tracked and highlight another important
point. Most of the data in this space
focuses on new vehicles (for the ABS
this is because used sales are a transfer

between households that ‘cancels out’ for
the sector as a whole). However, industry
estimates show used sales are both more
common and have been tracking a weaker
profile – the card data suggests used sales
are down at least 3%yr (finer estimates
putting the figure closer to 8%yr).
• Age-groups offer another insightful lens.
Activity has been much firmer across
older age groups, particularly in the ‘new
vehicle’ segment where they are more
prominent (accounting for 42% of all

activity) and activity is up on a year ago.
This group has notably stronger balance
sheets and is low exposure to interest
rate rises. In contrast, vehicle spend has
been contracting materially across other
age-groups, particularly used car spend
by the ‘mortgage belt’ 35-54 year olds.
Vehicle-related card activity highlights diverging trends

WESTPAC ECONOMICS 10
About the Westpac card data indicators
The indicators presented in this report are based on the millions of credit and debit card
transactions processed every day. Transactions covering over ten million merchants are
classified into over 700 categories. These are in turn grouped into higher level aggregates

that provide a timely guide to wider economic trends.
The main focus of these indicators is consumer spending. Where possible, we have sought
to exclude ‘non spending’ transactions such as: money transfers; tax payments; loan
repayments; charitable donations; and superannuation contributions.
It should also be noted that these indicators will also be affected by shifts between card and
non-card transactions. This was a significant factor during the COVID–19 pandemic – health

concerns about the use of physical cash leading to significantly higher use of cards vs cash,
particularly where contact–less transactions are available. Transaction flows also include
reversals/refunds which were also a significant phenomenon during the onset of COVID,
especially in areas such as travel.
All transaction data is compiled at a highly aggregated level so that individual customer or
merchant data is never revealed.
Index construction

The key metrics used in this report are indexes of spending–related card activity where the
base of 100 is average activity in 2019. As an example, if transaction flows are 5% above their
average level in 2019, the index read for the period is 105. If flows in a subsequent period are
8% above the average level in 2019, the index read for this period is 108. Growth between the
two periods can be calculated simply as the change between the two index reads, i.e. 2.9%.

All measures are adjusted for regular seasonality. Weekly estimates are generated using
the US Bureau of Labor’s MoveReg weekly seasonal adjustment program. Note that in some
cases, high levels of volatility during the COVID period mean it is not possible to produce
seasonally adjusted estimates for some historical periods.
Also, note that previous versions of this report used different approaches to seasonal
adjustment and measurement more generally. This means Index reads are sometimes not

directly comparable. See the ‘About the Westpac card data indicators’ sections from earlier
reports to more detail.
Classifications
Note that the measures used for card data and in this report do not align completely with
the those used in official ABS statistics, including the ABS household spending indicator,
ABS retail trade survey and ABS estimate of consumer spending published in the national
accounts. There are a range of differences including around both coverage and classification.

As such, the card data should be treated as broadly indicative.
The transaction data is grouped into 26 categories that are then combined into four main as
follows:
Discretionary goods: alcoholic beverages, tobacco, clothing & footwear, furnishings &
household equipment, household appliances, vehicle–related, recreational & cultural goods,
newspaper, books & stationery, and other personal effects.
Discretionary services: transport services (part), recreational & cultural services, gambling,

catering services, accommodation services, other personal care, insurance & financial
services, other services.
Essential goods: food & non alcoholic beverages, medical products, appliances & equipment,
and operation of personal transport equipment.
Essential services: electricity, gas & other fuels, health services, transport services (part),
communications and education.
The report also uses two additional classifications:
MHSI/Retail: based on the extent to which categories cover activity that is in scope for the

ABS monthly household spending indicator and ABS retail trade survey.

我們深度分析  Marvell 併購  Lumentum 將產⽣的綜效，雙⽅投資⼈
為何都不看好，未來 5 年財務衝擊及 outside the box thinking
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS
NOV 2 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP

What’s new?
Marvell Technology and Lumentum on Nov 2, 2026 announced that
they have entered into a deﬁnitive agreement for Marvell to acquire
Lumentum in an all-stock transaction. Under the terms of the
agreement, Lumentum stockholders will receive a ﬁxed exchange
ratio of 4.5 shares of Marvell common stock for each share of
Lumentum common stock they own. The transaction has an implied
enterprise value of $115 billion for Lumentum, representing
approximately $1,132 per share of Lumentum common stock and a

premium of approximately 23.9% based on each company’s
unaﬀected closing share price as of October 28, 2026, the last
trading day before market speculation regarding a potential
transaction began. Post-closing, current Marvell stockholders will
own approximately 67% of the combined company on a fully diluted
basis, while Lumentum stockholders will own approximately 33%.
但⾃從併購消息提前傳出來， Marvell 股價連續重挫，公司於 11 ⽉ 2 ⽇  收盤
171.41 美元，⽽  Lumentum 每股股東只能拿到 771.345 美元，這⽐起

Lumentum 收盤價 684.4 美元，溢價⼤幅縮⽔到 12.7% ，也⽐上週討論到的
併購溢價⼤幅縮⽔了 32% ，明顯表⽰ Marvell 及  Lumentum 的投資⼈都不看

### Page 2

好這次併購，到底是綜效不⾜，價格太⾼，未來 5 年財務預測衝擊⼤，還是擔
⼼  Marvell 買  Lumentum 在 AI 基礎建設投資週期頂點？
The synergies
Marvell 的核⼼意圖在於從單純的晶⽚設計商，升級為  AI 資料中⼼光電互連
（ Optical Interconnect ）的垂直整合巨擘：
綜效⼀：取得  InP 雷射製造能⼒，控制  CPO 的關鍵供應瓶頸
這可能是本次併購最有戰略價值的⼀部分。當  AI 叢集算⼒從  800G 邁向
1.6T 、 3.2T 甚⾄到  CPO （共同封裝光學元件）時代，⾼速傳輸的功耗與延遲
瓶頸已轉移⾄光電轉換。 Marvell 握有頂尖的  PAM4 DSP 、 TIA/Driver 及
ASIC 交換晶⽚，但⽋缺關鍵的光源硬體。 Lumentum 則掌握化合物半導體
（ InP 磷化銦⾃有晶圓廠）、連續波（ CW ）⾼功率雷射與  OCS （光電路交換）
技術。合併後能實現從數位、類⽐到光⼦端的全鏈路協同設計（ Co-

design ）。
對  CPO ⽽⾔， ASIC 及交換晶⽚只是其中⼀部分。完整⽅案仍需要：
⾼速電訊號處理與光電轉換。
CW Laser 或外部雷射光源（ ELS ）。
光調變器、接收器與光引擎。
⾼密度封裝、光纖耦合及散熱設計。
付費訂閱戶可分享我們對於四⼤綜效分析，雙⽅投資⼈
為何都不看好，在擔⼼什麼？對 Marvell 2027-2031
年的財務預測衝擊及 outside the box thinking
Subscribed
Marvell 買下  Lumentum ，相當於將原本必須外購的關鍵光源能⼒納⼊同⼀
家公司。這有機會改善設計整合、可靠度、功耗及量產交期，也降低受到外部

### Page 3

雷射供應商產能限制的⾵險。但應注意， CPO 不⼀定要求雷射與交換  ASIC 封
裝在同⼀顆晶⽚上；外置雷射仍然是重要的架構選項。
Source: Andrew Lu on global semis/techs
綜效⼆：從銷售  DSP ，升級到銷售完整  Optical Engine
以傳統  800G/1.6T 光收發器為例， Marvell 主要提供電⼦端  DSP 與相關類
⽐晶⽚，⽽  Lumentum 提供光源、光學器件及部分收發器解決⽅案。合併
後， Marvell 可以直接與客戶共同設計光電互連系統，⽽不僅是提供分離式零
件。這個差異的財務意義，是提⾼每條光連結的可取得價值（ Revenue
Content per Optical Link ），⽽⾮只靠光連結出貨量增⻑。尤其在未來  3.2T
光模組、 NPO 與  CPO 世代，系統設計越緊密，晶⽚、光引擎與雷射之間的聯
合驗證價值就越⾼。不過這並不保證客戶會購買整套⽅案。⼤型雲端業者可能
仍採取多供應商策略，並⾃⾏主導系統設計。

綜效三：正⾯挑戰  Broadcom 的  AI 互連⽣態系
過去  Marvell 與  Broadcom 的競爭，主要集中在  Custom ASIC 、 SerDes 、
DSP 、交換晶⽚及網路互連。併⼊  Lumentum 後，競爭範圍將延伸⾄雷射、
光引擎與  OCS ，這場競爭的核⼼不再只是「誰的  DSP 晶⽚⽐較便宜」，⽽是
誰能⽤較低的  pJ/bit 功耗與更可靠的光學系統，協助客戶建造更⼤規模的  AI
Cluster 。然⽽， Broadcom 仍有交換晶⽚市占、客戶關係與成熟網路軟硬體
⽣態系優勢。收購  Lumentum 不代表  Marvell ⽴即追平  Broadcom 的市場
地位。

### Page 4

Source: Andrew Lu on global semis/techs
綜效四：將雷射供應保障轉變成  Custom ASIC 的競爭籌碼
假設  Google 、 Amazon 或其他  Hyperscaler 未來發出新的  Custom AI
ASIC ，其評估標準可能不再只是算⼒、晶⽚價格與  TSMC 產能，⽽是整套  AI
Rack 的：
1. XPU 運算能⼒。
2. Scale-up / Scale-out 頻寬。
3. 光連結功耗與訊號完整性。
4. 雷射供應保障。
5. 整機櫃部署成本及可靠性。
Marvell 如果能同時提供  ASIC 設計與有保障的光學供應鏈，就可能提⾼其在
客戶系統架構設計中的參與度。
Why both Marvell and Lumentum investors worry?
1. 33% 的股權稀釋
全股票收購⼤幅膨脹股本： 交易完成後， Lumentum 原股東將持有合併
公司約  33% 股權， Marvell 既有股東僅剩約  67% 。

EPS 稀釋陷阱： 根據股權結構倒推， Marvell 現有業務每賺  100 美
元， Lumentum 加上淨綜效必須貢獻⾄少約  49 美元的獲利，才能勉強
打平原有股東的每股盈餘（ EPS ）。若  Lumentum 獲利未達爆發性預
期，既有股東權益將被嚴重攤薄。
2. 在週期⾼點買在「天價估值」
Lumentum 股價在收購前已因  AI 光模組需求⼤漲，交易估值隱含⾼達  35 倍
以上的遠期本益⽐。市場質疑  Marvell 是在  AI 基礎設施週期的估值頂峰，溢
價收購⼀家具有⾼度資本⽀出（ CapEx ）與重資產製造屬性的光學元件廠。
Fabless IC 設計公司併⼊化合物半導體晶圓廠，後續擴產將持續吞噬⾃由現⾦
流（ FCF ）。 Lumentum 的  AI 光通訊業務雖有結構性需求，但仍與超⼤型雲

### Page 5

端客戶  CapEx 、光模組需求及光學庫存循環⾼度相關。合併並不必然提⾼收
⼊來源的分散程度，反⽽⼤幅增加對  AI 基建景氣循環的曝險。
3. 漫⻑的交割期（ 2028 上半年）與反壟斷⾵險
官⽅預計交易要到  2028 年上半年才能完成，橫跨近兩年時間。
中國監管變數： 光學元件與通訊雷射⾼度牽動全球雲端與網通供應鏈，
Lumentum 在中國有廣泛業務與供應鏈佈局，這項超⼤型交易極可能遭
遇中國反壟斷機構（ SAMR ）的漫⻑審查與嚴苛附加條件。兩年內若  AI
資本⽀出週期降溫， Marvell 將承受巨⼤的合約與套牢⾵險。
4. 客戶中⽴性⽡解（ Supplier Neutrality ）與競業威脅
晶⽚客戶轉單⾵險： 原本向  Marvell 採購  DSP 晶⽚的光模組廠商（例
如旭創、新易盛等），或使⽤  Lumentum 雷射的網通巨頭（如  Cisco 、
Broadcom 陣營⽣態），可能因  Marvell 跨⼊整機 / 光模組⾃產⾃銷⽽產
⽣排擠與防備，甚⾄引發客戶轉向中⽴競爭對⼿（如  Coherent ）。

商業合作  vs. 資本併購： 雙⽅原本在  2026 年初就已透過商業聯盟共同
展⽰  OCS 技術，投資⼈質疑為何不能以商業代⼯或授權合作，⽽必須付
出千億美元代價冒險合體。
Financial impacts
我們根據彭博社分析師的預測， Lumentum 不管在營收，⽑利，還是
GAAP/non-GAAP 營業利潤及調整後 EPS 的 5 年（ 2026-2031 ）複合增⻑率
皆明顯優於 Marvell ， GAAP/non-GAAP 的營業利潤率也優於  Marvell ，加上
管理階層給出了每年超過  10 億美元的  Run-rate 成本綜效⽬標，並預期在併
購完成後⾸年（ Year 1 post-close ）即可實現  Non-GAAP EPS 的增厚
（ Accretive ）。但  Lumentum 在營收，⽑利，營業利潤的合併後貢獻⽐例皆
明顯不⾜股權稀釋⽐例的 33% ，這讓 Marvell 合併後的  adj EPS 低於併購前
市場預期 adj EPS 達 5-10 個百分點。外加超過 10 倍股東權益淨值的龐⼤溢價

將逐年攤提， Marvell 合併  Lumentum 之後的 GAAP EPS 也將明顯低於之前
預期。

### Page 6

Source: Andrew Lu on global semis/techs, Bloomberg
Outside the box thinking
第⼀， Marvell 正從  Fabless 模式 , 轉變成帶有重要製造資產的半導體集團
⻑期以來， Marvell 的商業模式主要仰賴外部先進晶圓代⼯及封裝⽣態系。
Lumentum 則有⾃有  InP 及其他化合物半導體製造能⼒。這表⽰合併後公司
將同時擁有⾼階  ASIC 設計與特殊半導體製造資產。好處是能控制稀缺製程產
能；壞處是必須承擔設備投資、產能利⽤率與製造良率⾵險。市場可能必須重
新評估  Marvell 是否仍適合享有純⾼成⻑  Fabless ASIC 公司的估值倍數。
第⼆， CPO 將使  Optical Connectivity 的商業模式變得更接近客製化
ASIC

### Page 7

傳統光模組由多家零件商提供標準化產品；未來部分⾼階  CPO 光引擎可能需
要與交換  ASIC 、 XPU 以及系統架構⾼度共同設計。如果這個⽅向成⽴，競爭
將逐漸從單純⽐價格，轉向：
誰掌握系統架構設計。
誰擁有完整  IP 與可靠度驗證能⼒。
誰能提供有保障的量產產能。
誰能在功耗、頻寬與單位成本之間取得最佳組合。
Marvell 可能希望將過去與  Hyperscaler 合作  Custom ASIC 的經驗，移植
到  Optical Connectivity 業務。但我們認為應區分兩個市場：傳統
Pluggable Optics 仍可能保持多供應商與模組化結構，⽽⾼度客製化  CPO
的整合優勢才可能真正擴⼤。
第三，這筆併購也可能是  Marvell 對光通訊獲利分配的⼀種防禦
當  1.6T 、 3.2T 及  CPO 時代來臨，單⼀光連結的成本與價值分配可能改變。
假如  DSP 成本占⽐下降，⽽雷射、光引擎、先進封裝與光學耦合占⽐提升，
原本⾼度依賴  DSP 的公司即使維持市占率，也不⼀定能取得最⼤經濟價值。

Document 454: Why AT1 still matters in 2027

Source PDF: all report pdf/Why AT1 still matters in 2027.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 12
